        const forecastRows = [{"date": "2025-05-01", "consumption": 3.13, "status": "Provisional"}, {"date": "2025-05-02", "consumption": 3.13, "status": "Provisional"}, {"date": "2025-05-03",         const nextMonthRows = [{"date": "2025-05-01", "consumption": 3.13, "status": "Predicted"}, {"date": "2025-05-02", "consumption": 3.13, "status": "Predicted"}, {"date": "2025-05-03", {"date": "2025-05-26", "consumption": 3.15, "status": "Predicted"}, {"date": "2025-05-27", "consumption": 3.15, "status": "Predicted"}, {"date": "2025-05-28", "consumption": 3.15, "status": "Predicted"}, {"date": "2025-05-29", "consumption": 3.15, "status": "Predicted"}, {"date": "2025-05-30", "consumption": 3.15, "status": "Predicted"}, {"date": "2025-05-31", "consumption": 3.16, "status": "Predicted"}];

        const applianceLabels = applianceRows.map(item => item.appliance);
        const applianceData = applianceRows.map(item => item.consumption === 'N/A' ? null : Number(item.consumption));

        if (applianceLabels.length) {
            new Chart(document.getElementById('applianceChart'), {
                type: 'bar',
                data: {
                    labels: applianceLabels,
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Consumption',
                        data: applianceData,
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.75)',
                        borderRadius: 8
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    maintainAspectRatio: false,
                    scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true } },
                    plugins: { legend: { display: false } }
                }
            });
        }

        const historyLabels = historicalRows.map(item => item.date);
        const historyValues = historicalRows.map(item => Number(item.consumption));
        if (historyLabels.length) {
            new Chart(document.getElementById('historicalChart'), {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: historyLabels,
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Historical consumption',
                        data: historyValues,
                        borderColor: '#60a5fa',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.2)',
                        tension: 0.35,
                        fill: true
                    }]
                },
                options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false }
            });
        }

        const forecastDates = forecastRows.map(item => item.date);
        const forecastValues = forecastRows.map(item => Number(item.consumption));
        if (forecastDates.length) {
            new Chart(document.getElementById('forecastChart'), {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: forecastDates,
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Forecast',
                        data: forecastValues,
                        borderColor: '#34d399',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.25)',
                        tension: 0.3,
                        fill: true
                    }]
                },
                options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false }
            });
        }

        const nextMonthDates = nextMonthRows.map(item => item.date);
        const nextMonthValues = nextMonthRows.map(item => Number(item.consumption));
        if (nextMonthDates.length) {
            new Chart(document.getElementById('nextMonthChart'), {
                type: 'bar',
                data: {
                    labels: nextMonthDates,
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Next month forecast',
                        data: nextMonthValues,
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.75)',
                        borderRadius: 4
                    }]
                },
                options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, scales: { x: { display: false } } }
            });
        }

        const tableBody = document.getElementById('forecastTableBody');
        if (forecastRows.length && tableBody) {
            forecastRows.slice(0, 12).forEach(row => {
                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                const avgValue = historicalRows.length ? historicalRows.reduce((sum, item) => sum + Number(item.consumption), 0) / historicalRows.length : 0;
                const diff = avgValue ? ((Number(row.consumption) - avgValue) / avgValue) * 100 : 0;
                tr.innerHTML = `
                    <td>${row.date}</td>
                    <td>${Number(row.consumption).toFixed(2)} kWh</td>
                    <td>${row.status || 'Provisional'}</td>
                    <td>${diff >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${diff.toFixed(2)}%</td>
                `;
                tableBody.appendChild(tr);
            });
        }

        const balanceAppliance = document.getElementById('balanceAppliance');
        const balanceOutput = document.getElementById('balanceOutput');
        const averageUsage = historicalRows.length ? historicalRows.reduce((sum, item) => sum + Number(item.consumption), 0) / historicalRows.length : 0;
        const forecastValue = forecastRows.length ? forecastRows.reduce((sum, item) => sum + Number(item.consumption), 0) / forecastRows.length : 0;

        function updateBalance() {
            if (!balanceAppliance || !balanceOutput) return;
            const appliance = balanceAppliance.value;
            const deficit = Math.max(forecastValue - averageUsage, 0);
            const message = `Your forecast is already ${deficit.toFixed(2)} kWh above your historical average. To increase ${appliance} while staying close to your normal usage, reduce flexible appliance consumption by approximately ${deficit.toFixed(2)} kWh and focus on higher-impact loads in the household.`;
            balanceOutput.textContent = message;
        }

        if (balanceAppliance) {
            balanceAppliance.addEventListener('change', updateBalance);
            updateBalance();
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
